componentsCooling.citable.question	Why does cooling matter in a home-use IPL device?
componentsCooling.citable.paragraph	Cooling is a key design factor for home-use IPL devices because it improves skin-contact comfort and helps manage thermal load during repeated flashes. Premium configurations often use a sapphire ice plate or a semiconductor (TEC) cooling module to pull heat away from the treatment window, with some designs targeting skin-contact temperatures around ~10°C. Better thermal management can make higher-energy settings feel more tolerable and can reduce the risk of user discomfort when treating sensitive areas, which supports better session consistency. Cooling choices also affect industrial design, power consumption, assembly complexity, and cost, so the right system depends on brand positioning and target-market requirements. For OEM/ODM programs, iShine integrates the cooling approach with lamp, filter, temperature sensing, and safety interlocks so the final device maintains stable performance across production batches and supports verification and documentation for distribution.

componentsPage.citable.question	What are the core components of a home-use IPL device?
componentsPage.citable.paragraph	A home-use IPL device is built from several core subsystems that directly affect safety, comfort, and performance. The flash source is typically a xenon lamp cartridge, engineered for stable output and high flash counts (commonly 500,000-999,000 flashes depending on platform). Optical filters shape the emitted spectrum by blocking unwanted wavelengths (including UV) and selecting bands suited to hair-reduction positioning, with common cut-on options such as 550 nm or 570 nm in supported configurations. A cooling system manages skin-contact comfort and heat load; options include sapphire ice plates and semiconductor (TEC) cooling designs used in premium builds, with some designs targeting skin-contact temperatures around ~10°C. The power supply provides consistent voltage delivery and protects the device through electrical design choices such as insulation, grounding, and thermal safeguards, typically supporting global input ranges like 100-240V AC for international launches. For private-label programs, these components are selected as a matched platform so the final device can meet target-market requirements, maintain consistent batch performance, and support documentation and verification during scale production.

componentsFilter.citable.question	What do optical filters do in an IPL device?
componentsFilter.citable.paragraph	Optical filters in an IPL device shape the light output by blocking unwanted wavelengths and transmitting bands that match the intended treatment positioning. In practical terms, filters help reduce UV exposure and can tune the effective wavelength range delivered through the treatment window (for example, common cut-on options like 550 nm, 570 nm, or 600 nm in supported configurations). Filter design influences safety margin across skin tones, perceived comfort, and the balance between efficacy and tolerability at different energy settings. For OEM/ODM production, filter selection is treated as part of a matched optical stack with the lamp tube and window materials, because small changes can affect output consistency and verification results. iShine supports filter configuration within private-label programs to align performance targets with target-market documentation and manufacturing repeatability.

componentsLamp.citable.question	What is an IPL lamp cartridge and why does it matter?
componentsLamp.citable.paragraph	An IPL lamp cartridge is the flash source that generates intense pulsed light in a home-use IPL device. Most platforms use a xenon flash lamp tube paired with optical filtering so the emitted spectrum targets hair pigment while reducing unwanted wavelengths such as UV. Lamp design affects pulse stability, energy consistency, heat load, and expected flash count across the device lifetime (commonly 500,000-999,000 flashes depending on platform). For private-label programs, a well-specified lamp cartridge helps reduce batch-to-batch variation and supports repeatable performance positioning, because the lamp is central to how energy is delivered through the treatment window. iShine integrates lamp selection with filters, cooling, and safety interlocks so the final device can be manufactured consistently and supported with verification and documentation for target-market requirements.
componentsPower.breadcrumb.components	Components
componentsPower.header.title	Power Supply
componentsPower.header.description	Efficient and safe power supply systems for IPL devices
componentsPower.intro.title	The Power Supply Is Where Safety and Compliance Begin
componentsPower.intro.paragraph	Every IPL flash begins with the power supply converting wall power into a precise, repeatable DC output. That single conversion decides how consistent your pulses are, how safely the device runs, and whether it clears the certification bar in your target market. It is also where engineering meets a commercial decision: which adapter grade your sales channel actually requires. We help brands get both right — from standard certified adapters for e-commerce to medical-grade units for regulated retail — with plug styles and certifications matched to your market.

componentsPower.citable.question	What does the power supply do in an IPL device?
componentsPower.citable.paragraph	The power supply is the electrical subsystem that enables stable, repeatable energy delivery for IPL pulses. It converts AC mains input into the controlled DC output that drives the lamp, cooling modules, and control electronics of an at-home IPL hair removal device, supporting global input ranges like 100–240V AC for international distribution. iShine's adapters deliver DC output typically at 12V 2.5A, 12V 3.0A, or 12V 4.0A, matched to each model's energy level and cooling configuration. The adapter grade you need depends on where you sell: brands entering retail chains and supermarkets face strong regulation, so a medical-grade power adapter is mandatory and typically costs about US$2.5 more than a standard-grade adapter; brands selling through Shopify or a self-built DTC storefront are under lighter regulation and can use a normal certified adapter. iShine supplies certified adapters in UK, US, AU, EU, and JP plug styles, with support for additional markets including Argentina and Japan. Private customization is available — for example, a power cord finished in skin-friendly material — so the adapter aligns with your brand, packaging, and target region.

servicesPage.index.citableEyebrow	Citable Summary
servicesPage.index.citableQuestion	What is included in iShine's IPL manufacturing services?
servicesPage.index.citableParagraph	iShine provides end-to-end IPL manufacturing services for private-label programs, covering OEM/ODM development, product design, production and assembly, packaging, and export logistics. A typical engagement starts with requirements and positioning, then moves into engineering decisions such as cooling approach (including premium sapphire contact cooling targeting ~10°C in supported configurations), energy range, flash window sizing, wavelength filtering, and safety interlocks. Platform-based prototype sampling can often begin in 1-4 weeks depending on scope, while programs involving tooling, electronics, firmware, or compliance work add time for validation, documentation, and production planning. Packaging support includes branded cartons, inserts, manuals, labeling, and accessory selection aligned to retail, distributor, or e-commerce channels, with options such as manual localization and channel-ready labeling. Delivery support includes export documents and shipping coordination (FOB Shenzhen, with CIF options) by sea, air, or courier based on timeline and destination requirements.

servicesPage.oemOdm.citable.heading	What does OEM vs ODM mean for an IPL device launch?
servicesPage.oemOdm.citable.body	OEM (Original Equipment Manufacturing) means iShine manufactures an IPL device to a partner's defined specifications, such as target energy range, cooling configuration, wavelength filtering, cosmetics, packaging, and labeling. ODM (Original Design Manufacturing) adds product development on top of manufacturing, using an existing platform as a base or creating a new design with industrial design, engineering, prototyping, and validation before scale production. Both models can support private-label requirements like logo printing, multi-color options, manual localization, plug and adapter configuration, and accessory selection. A typical program includes requirements alignment, prototype sampling (often starting in 1-4 weeks for platform-based builds depending on scope), performance and safety verification, documentation preparation for target markets, and production planning for volume delivery. Timelines vary by customization depth: branding-only projects move faster, while projects involving tooling, electronics, firmware, or compliance work require longer schedules. The goal is a market-ready IPL product that can be produced consistently and shipped globally.

servicesPage.productDesign.citable.heading	What does IPL product design include in an OEM/ODM project?
servicesPage.productDesign.citable.body	IPL product design in an OEM/ODM project covers both aesthetics and engineering decisions that affect usability, safety, manufacturability, and brand positioning. It typically includes industrial design for ergonomics and premium look-and-feel, UI/UX for controls and indicators, and component-level planning such as cooling approach, treatment window sizing, and sensor integration. Design work also considers packaging and unboxing so the product matches retail or DTC expectations. A strong design phase reduces later production risk by aligning materials, tolerances, and assembly constraints early, which improves consistency at scale. iShine supports design programs from concept exploration through 3D modeling, prototyping, testing, and iterative refinement, so the final device can be produced repeatably and documented for target markets.

servicesPage.productionAssembly.hero.cta	Discuss Production Needs
servicesPage.productionAssembly.citable.heading	How is an IPL device produced at scale for private-label?
servicesPage.productionAssembly.citable.body	Scaled IPL device production for private-label typically follows a structured flow: verified component sourcing, pre-production line setup, pilot runs to validate yield and test stations, and then mass production with ongoing quality monitoring. Key checkpoints include electrical safety tests, optical output verification, pulse stability and energy calibration, temperature and cooling checks, and final functional tests of sensors and user interface. For target markets, documentation and traceability processes are maintained alongside production so batches remain consistent and auditable. iShine aligns production planning with the selected lamp, filter, cooling, and power subsystems to reduce variation between lots and support repeatable performance. This approach helps brands launch reliably, scale quantities, and maintain consistent customer experience across shipments.

servicesPage.packagingLogistics.citable.heading	What is included in packaging and logistics for an IPL brand launch?
servicesPage.packagingLogistics.citable.body	Packaging and logistics for an IPL brand launch cover both the customer-facing unboxing experience and the practical protections needed for global delivery. Programs typically include branded cartons, inserts, manuals, labeling, and accessory selection, plus protective structures designed to reduce damage during transit. For e-commerce, packaging is often optimized for compact size and durability; for retail, it is designed for shelf appeal and clear communication. Logistics support includes export documents, shipping coordination by sea or air, customs handling, and tracking, with routing choices based on cost, destination, and timeline requirements. iShine supports packaging workflows that align with OEM/ODM production schedules so products can ship consistently and arrive in market-ready condition.

servicesPage.noMoq.citable.heading	What does “No MOQ” mean for a private-label IPL launch?
servicesPage.noMoq.citable.body	“No MOQ” means you can start a branded IPL program with a smaller pilot quantity to validate your channel and messaging, then scale production through repeat orders instead of committing to large upfront inventory. A practical No MOQ setup includes clear branding requirements, defined packaging and manual content, stable bill of materials control, and a replenishment plan that supports predictable lead times as volume grows. The goal is to reduce launch risk while preserving a professional brand presentation.

servicesPage.boxCustom.citable.heading	What is included in box customization for an IPL brand?
servicesPage.boxCustom.citable.body	Box customization for an IPL brand typically includes custom carton dielines, print-ready artwork setup, insert design for protection, and channel requirements such as barcodes and labeling. A complete packaging program also considers transit durability, unboxing experience, and master-carton optimization to reduce freight cost and damage rate. The goal is packaging that sells, protects, and scales reliably.

aboutPages.overview.title	About iShine
aboutPages.overview.description	iShine is an IPL hair removal device manufacturer providing OEM and ODM services for beauty brands, from product design to global delivery.
aboutPages.overview.heroLabel	Company Overview
aboutPages.overview.heroDescription	Leading manufacturer of IPL hair removal devices, dedicated to helping brands succeed from concept to market.
aboutPages.overview.summaryTitle	What is iShine Technology Ltd.?
aboutPages.overview.summaryDescription	iShine Technology Ltd. is a B2B OEM/ODM manufacturer of home-use IPL hair removal devices that supports private-label programs for beauty brands and retailers. Founded in 2021 in Shenzhen, China, iShine provides end-to-end services across product design, prototyping, scalable production, packaging, compliance planning, and export delivery. The company references international market requirements such as FDA and CE pathways and operates with quality-system alignment including ISO 13485. iShine reports 500,000+ devices produced, and 50+ global client partnerships, shipping to 20+ countries across North America, Europe, and the Middle East. Its work focuses on manufacturing repeatability and brand-ready execution so partners can launch and scale IPL products with consistent quality.

aboutPages.manufacturing.summaryTitle	What manufacturing capabilities does iShine offer for IPL devices?
aboutPages.manufacturing.summaryDescription	iShine’s manufacturing capabilities for IPL devices cover the core steps required to move from a validated design to repeatable production and delivery. This includes controlled assembly workflows, sourcing and integration of key subsystems (lamp, filters, cooling, power), in-line testing, calibration, and final inspection to reduce variation across batches. Capability planning also extends to packaging execution and logistics coordination so finished products can ship in market-ready form. For OEM/ODM partners, these capabilities matter because they support consistent performance, stable quality outcomes, and smoother documentation and verification work when targeting multiple markets. The overall goal is to provide a scalable manufacturing foundation for private-label launches.

aboutPages.qualityControl.description	Multi-stage quality control for IPL devices: incoming inspection, in-process checks, functional testing, and pre-shipment audit. ISO 9001, CE, FDA certified.
aboutPages.qualityControl.heroSubtitle	Multi-stage inspection and testing to keep every batch stable, compliant, and retail-ready.
aboutPages.qualityControl.summaryTitle	How does iShine approach quality control for IPL devices?
aboutPages.qualityControl.summaryDescription	iShine approaches quality control for IPL devices as a multi-stage workflow designed to reduce variation and keep performance stable across production batches. Typical steps include incoming inspection of critical components, in-process verification during assembly, functional testing of device operation, and a final pre-shipment audit. QC checks often cover electrical safety, optical output verification, calibration of energy settings, thermal behavior at the treatment window, and general reliability testing. This quality-first approach helps OEM/ODM partners maintain consistent production outcomes while supporting documentation and verification needs for target markets. The intent is a retail-ready device experience where performance and safety checks are built into the process rather than treated as an afterthought.
aboutPages.qualityControl.philosophyTitle	Our QC Philosophy
aboutPages.qualityControl.philosophyParagraph	Quality is built into every step, not inspected at the end. Our process combines incoming inspection, in-process verification, functional testing, and a final pre-shipment audit to reduce variation and improve consistency across batches.
aboutPages.qualityControl.flowTitle	Typical QC Flow
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step1.title	Incoming Material Inspection
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step1.description	Verify key components and materials before production starts.
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step2.title	In-Process Checks
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step2.description	Quality checkpoints during assembly to catch variation early.
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step3.title	Functional & Safety Testing
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step3.description	Performance verification, safety compliance, and calibration.
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step4.title	Cosmetic Inspection
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step4.description	Appearance inspection aligned with retail expectations and standards.
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step5.title	Pre-Shipment Audit
aboutPages.qualityControl.flow.step5.description	Final review, packaging checks, and documentation alignment before shipment.
aboutPages.qualityControl.testingTitle	Testing Coverage

aboutPages.contactPage.summaryTitle	How do I start an IPL OEM/ODM inquiry with iShine?
aboutPages.contactPage.summaryDescription	To start an IPL OEM/ODM inquiry with iShine, share a short project brief including your target market, desired positioning, expected order quantity range, and timeline. Helpful details include the device concept (app-connected, cooling-focused, AI skin sensing, or a specific model reference), preferred packaging style, and any compliance requirements such as CE-marked configurations or documentation needs for FDA-related pathways. If you already have a BOM, reference photos, or competitor benchmarks, include them to reduce back-and-forth. After the brief is received, the next step is typically sample selection and a discussion of customization scope, MOQ, lead time, and documentation planning so the project can move into prototyping and production scheduling.

auxPages.faq.categories.generalAboutIshine.faqs.whoDoYouWorkWith.question	Who do you typically work with?
auxPages.faq.categories.generalAboutIshine.faqs.whoDoYouWorkWith.answer	We commonly support beauty brands, private label startups, distributors, sourcing teams, retailers, and market expansion teams that need reliable IPL product development and manufacturing support.
auxPages.faq.categories.generalAboutIshine.faqs.consumerAndProfessionalLines.question	Do you support both consumer and professional product lines?

auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.title	OEM / ODM Services
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.description	Questions about manufacturing models, customization scope, and project setup for branded device programs.
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.oemVsOdm.question	What is the difference between OEM and ODM services?
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.oemVsOdm.answer	OEM (Original Equipment Manufacturing) means we manufacture products based on your exact design specifications. ODM (Original Design Manufacturing) means we provide both design and manufacturing - you can select from our existing designs and customize them with your branding. Both options offer full customization of packaging, colors, and features.
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.minimumOrderQuantity.question	What is the minimum order quantity (MOQ)?
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.minimumOrderQuantity.answer	Our standard MOQ starts at 500 units for most products. For early-stage brands or pilot programs, we can discuss sample orders, validation runs, or phased launch planning based on project scope.
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.customizeDesignAndFeatures.question	Can you customize the device design and features?
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.customizeDesignAndFeatures.answer	Absolutely. We offer full customization including industrial design, color schemes, logo printing, custom packaging, firmware modifications, app integration, and feature customization such as energy levels, flash modes, and cooling systems. Our R&D team works closely with you to bring your vision to life.
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.conceptToDeliveryTimeline.question	How long does the OEM/ODM process take from concept to delivery?
auxPages.faq.categories.oemOdmServices.faqs.conceptToDeliveryTimeline.answer	Typical timelines range from 8-16 weeks depending on the level of customization. Simple branding projects can move faster, while projects involving industrial design changes, tooling, electronics, firmware, or compliance work require a longer schedule.
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.title	Product Customization & IPL Basics
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.description	Practical answers on features, treatment positioning, and product-use education that supports both sales and launch readiness.
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.typesOfIplDevices.question	What types of IPL devices does iShine manufacture?
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.typesOfIplDevices.answer	We manufacture IPL devices for hair removal, skin rejuvenation, and acne-care positioning. Programs can range from ready-to-brand models to more customized private label product platforms.
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.sapphireCoolingVsStandard.question	What is the difference between sapphire cooling and standard IPL systems?
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.sapphireCoolingVsStandard.answer	Sapphire cooling helps manage skin-contact comfort during treatment and can improve the perceived user experience. Standard IPL platforms may offer strong performance as well, but sapphire-based systems are often chosen for premium positioning and comfort-led marketing.
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.suitableForAllSkinTones.question	Are your IPL devices suitable for all skin tones?
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.suitableForAllSkinTones.answer	Most models are suitable for Fitzpatrick skin types I-V, depending on device configuration and safety settings. Compatibility should always be confirmed at the product level before launch claims or market rollout.
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.shapeProductStory.question	Can you help us shape the product story for our market?
auxPages.faq.categories.productCustomization.faqs.shapeProductStory.answer	Yes. We can support positioning discussions around feature sets, user comfort, treatment areas, packaging direction, and documentation so the final product aligns with your target customer and channel expectations.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.title	IPL Education & Safety (Consumer FAQs)
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.description	Customer-facing questions about how IPL works, typical usage patterns, comfort, and safety precautions. Useful for FAQ pages, manuals, and launch-ready support content.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whatIsIpl.question	What is IPL and how does an IPL handset reduce hair growth?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whatIsIpl.answer	IPL stands for Intense Pulsed Light. The handset emits pulses of filtered light that are absorbed by pigment in the hair. That energy converts to heat near the follicle, which can reduce hair regrowth over repeated sessions. With consistent use, many users see slower regrowth and finer hair density over time.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.isIplSafe.question	Is IPL hair removal safe? Can IPL cause cancer?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.isIplSafe.answer	IPL is widely used for hair reduction in both consumer and professional settings. Standard IPL hair removal devices use filtered light and are designed to minimize UV exposure; there is no good evidence that properly used IPL hair removal causes cancer. For safety, follow the instructions, avoid treating broken or irritated skin, and consult a clinician if you have a medical condition or take photosensitizing medication.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.doesIplHurt.question	Does IPL treatment hurt?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.doesIplHurt.answer	Most users describe IPL as a quick warm snap rather than pain. Higher energy settings can feel more intense on sensitive areas. Start at a low intensity, test a small patch, and increase gradually only if your skin tolerates the treatment comfortably.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whenWillISeeResults.question	When will I see results, and is IPL permanent?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whenWillISeeResults.answer	Many people notice reduced regrowth after a few sessions, with clearer results after a full course (often around 12 weekly sessions). IPL is generally described as long-term hair reduction rather than permanent hair removal. Maintenance sessions may be needed depending on hair type, hormones, and the treatment area.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whereOnBody.question	Where on the body can I use an IPL handset?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whereOnBody.answer	IPL is commonly used on legs, arms, underarms, bikini line, and parts of the face. Avoid treating above the cheekbones, and avoid inner intimate areas (including mucosal tissue). For sensitive areas like the bikini line and inner thighs, use a lower intensity level and follow the device instructions.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.howOften.question	How often should I use IPL?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.howOften.answer	A typical schedule is once per week for 12 consecutive weeks. Some routines allow up to twice weekly early on, but you should follow the instructions for your specific device configuration. After the initial course, many users do maintenance sessions every 4–12 weeks as needed.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.skinToneAndHairColor.question	Does IPL work for my skin tone and hair color?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.skinToneAndHairColor.answer	IPL works best when there is contrast between hair pigment and skin tone, because the light targets melanin in the hair. It may be less effective on very light blonde, red, or grey hair. Very dark or recently sunburned skin may carry a higher burn risk. Always follow the skin tone compatibility chart and guidance for the specific device configuration.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.voltageAndPlug.question	Will an IPL handset work in my country (voltage and plug type)?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.voltageAndPlug.answer	Most modern IPL handsets are designed for global use with dual-voltage power supplies (commonly 110V–240V). Plug types and adapters depend on the destination country and the final packaging configuration. Confirm the exact power spec and included plug type for the model you plan to launch.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.shaveBeforeUse.question	Do I need to shave before using IPL? What other hair removal methods are compatible?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.shaveBeforeUse.answer	Shaving is usually recommended because it keeps hair short at the surface while leaving the root available for light targeting. Waxing or tweezing removes the hair from the root and can reduce effectiveness during the treatment cycle. If you treat facial areas, follow device guidance; some users choose gentle dermaplaning beforehand.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.howToClean.question	How do I clean an IPL handset?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.howToClean.answer	Disconnect the device from power before cleaning. Wipe the surface with a clean, dry cloth and 70% isopropyl alcohol. Avoid acetone or abrasive cleaners, and never immerse the device or any parts in water.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whatIsLampMadeOf.question	What is the IPL lamp made of?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.whatIsLampMadeOf.answer	Many IPL handsets use a xenon flash lamp tube with optical filtering to reduce unwanted wavelengths such as UV. Lamp construction and filter design vary by model and configuration.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.ageToStart.question	At what age can I start IPL treatment?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.ageToStart.answer	Many brands recommend IPL for users aged 16+ because hair growth maturity often develops around that age. Anyone under 18 should use IPL only with parental guidance and professional advice.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.eyeProtection.question	Do I need eye protection when using IPL?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.eyeProtection.answer	Many handsets have skin-contact sensors and will not flash unless the treatment window is pressed against skin. Even so, avoid staring at the flash and consider protective eyewear if you are sensitive to bright light.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.pregnantOrBreastfeeding.question	Is it safe to use IPL while pregnant or breastfeeding?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.pregnantOrBreastfeeding.answer	There is limited direct research for at-home IPL use during pregnancy or breastfeeding. As a precaution, many brands recommend avoiding IPL during pregnancy and breastfeeding. If unsure, consult your clinician.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.frecklesMolesTattoos.question	Is it safe to do IPL over freckles, moles, or tattoos?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.frecklesMolesTattoos.answer	Avoid treating directly over dark moles, very dark freckles, or tattoos because darker pigment can absorb more energy and increase burn risk. If you have areas you want to treat near these spots, treat around them and follow device guidance.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.beforeOrAfterTanning.question	Can I use IPL before or after tanning?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.beforeOrAfterTanning.answer	If you have recently tanned, wait about two weeks before using IPL. After IPL, many guidelines recommend avoiding strong sun exposure for at least 48 hours. Always follow the instructions for your device and your skin sensitivity.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.hurtsOrBurning.question	Using IPL hurts or feels like burning. What should I do?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.hurtsOrBurning.answer	Stop the session and lower the intensity next time. Common causes of discomfort include recent sun exposure, treating on skin that is too dark for the setting, using an intensity that is too high, not shaving beforehand, pressing too hard, repeating flashes on the same spot, or using lotions/gels that are not recommended. If pain persists or you suspect a device fault, discontinue use and seek support.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.flashModes.question	What flash modes are commonly available on IPL handsets?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.flashModes.answer	Many IPL devices provide modes such as Stamp (single flash per press), Auto (automatic flashing when in contact with skin), and Glide (continuous movement over larger areas). Available modes and recommended technique depend on the model and safety design.
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.recurringCostsAndLifetime.question	Are there recurring costs and how long does an IPL handset last?
auxPages.faq.categories.iplEducationSafety.faqs.recurringCostsAndLifetime.answer	Most home-use IPL handsets are designed with high flash counts and do not require refills. Flash count and lifetime vary by model and configuration; many devices are engineered for multi-year use under typical treatment schedules.

auxPages.faq.categories.complianceQuality.description	Answers focused on testing, documentation, certifications, and manufacturing control for regulated or quality-sensitive markets.

auxPages.faq.categories.complianceQuality.faqs.certifications.answer	Product certifications depend on the model and market. Common support areas include CE, FDA-related documentation pathways, IEC safety standards, and quality-system documentation required for target-region review.

auxPages.privacy.sections.dnt.body1	Most web browsers and some mobile operating systems and mobile applications include a Do-Not-Track (“DNT”) feature or setting you can activate to signal your privacy preference not to have data about your online browsing activities monitored and collected. At this stage no uniform technology standard for recognizing and implementing DNT signals has been finalized. As such, we do not currently respond to DNT browser signals. If a standard for online tracking is adopted that we must follow in the future, we will inform you about that practice in a revised version of this privacy notice.

clientPage.catalogue.faq[1].question	What matters most for "painless" positioning?
clientPage.catalogue.faq[1].answer	Cooling approach and thermal design. If your brand positioning is comfort-first, prioritize models with ice plate / sapphire cooling and validate performance in sampling.

clientPage.catalogue.faq[3].question	What should we customize first: device logo, box, or manual?
clientPage.catalogue.faq[3].answer	Start with the brand elements that customers see first (logo and box), then ensure the manual reduces misuse and support tickets. Align these with your channel requirements.

clientPage.catalogue.faq[4].question	Can we start with a small pilot order?
clientPage.catalogue.faq[4].answer	Yes. Many private-label programs can start with a smaller pilot order to validate demand and positioning, then scale with predictable replenishment.

clientPage.projectSnapshot	Project Snapshot
clientPage.caseStudyCitableSummaryTitle	What is this IPL OEM/ODM case study about?
clientPage.caseStudyCitableSummaryBody	This case study describes how iShine supported a partner's IPL device program from requirements and market constraints through manufacturing execution. The project scope typically includes OEM/ODM alignment, feature and configuration decisions, brand integration across device and packaging, and compliance planning for the target market described in the project snapshot. Execution commonly combines prototyping, validation, and scaled production planning, followed by logistics coordination for distribution. Results are measured through outcomes such as improved time-to-market, stronger positioning, and supply chain reliability, alongside device-level differentiators like cooling, smart sensing, or performance tuning depending on the program goals. The intent is to show a repeatable workflow for private-label IPL launches where product experience and manufacturing consistency are managed together.

develoContent.blogDetail.breadcrumb.ariaLabel	Breadcrumb

develoContent.blogDetail.category	MAGENTO
develoContent.blogDetail.title	8 Tips to Optimise Magento Speed and Performance
develoContent.blogDetail.description	Practical ways to make your Magento store faster, smoother, and more profitable
develoContent.blogDetail.author	By Bethany Wood
develoContent.blogDetail.date	February 19, 2026
develoContent.blogDetail.readTime	5 min read
develoContent.blogDetail.updated	Last updated: February 19, 2026
develoContent.blogDetail.share.label	Share this article
develoContent.blogDetail.share.twitter	Share on Twitter
develoContent.blogDetail.share.linkedin	Share on LinkedIn
develoContent.blogDetail.share.facebook	Share on Facebook
develoContent.blogDetail.authorRole	Marketing Manager
develoContent.blogDetail.authorBio	Beth has worked in marketing since 2016, delivering creative, conversion-focused campaigns across multiple industries. She specialises in social media marketing, branding, email marketing, and copywriting. As Develo's Marketing Manager within the commercial department, Beth is responsible for managing and delivering marketing activity that supports business growth. She enjoys taking projects from concept to delivery, shaping ideas into campaigns that drive measurable results.
develoContent.blogDetail.authorCompany	Develo is a leading Magento agency and eCommerce web development company based in Birmingham, in the UK, serving clients globally since 2010.
develoContent.blogDetail.cta.text	Need help optimising your Magento store? Let's talk.
develoContent.blogDetail.cta.button	Book a call
develoContent.blogDetail.body.para1Before	Optimising Magento site speed and performance should be at the top of any business owner's priority list. Why? Because with today's customers, every second counts. In fact,
develoContent.blogDetail.body.para1After	when pages take longer than three seconds to load. Slow sites don't just irritate customers, they also hurt your Magento site's user experience (UX) and SEO rankings, especially now that site speed is a key ranking factor in Google's Core Web Vitals testing.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.para2	Magento site optimisation helps avoid these issues, in turn boosting your sales and conversions. In what follows, we've put together 8 essential tips to help you (or your developer!) optimise Magento speed and ensure your site is built to perform, however your eCommerce store is set up.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.section1Title	How can you test your Magento speed and performance with Core Web Vitals?
develoContent.blogDetail.body.section1ParaBefore	Before making any changes, we advise running a Core Web Vitals test. Ask your developer to check for speed issues and improvements using tools like
develoContent.blogDetail.body.section1ParaMiddle	or
develoContent.blogDetail.body.section1ParaAfter	. These give you a baseline to measure progress and highlight any areas holding your site back.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.section2Title	Once you're done, follow these 8 Magento performance optimisation tips.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip1Title	1. Enable All Magento Cache Types and Use Varnish
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip1Para1	Magento comes with several built-in caching options. Enabling these is one of the quickest ways to boost site performance. Our recommendation? Varnish, a powerful caching tool that significantly reduces page load times by storing a version of your website in memory.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip1Para2	This means repeat visitors won't need to reload every page element, saving you time, server resources, and reducing abandoned purchases.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip2Title	2. Enable Redis for Session and Cache Storage
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip2Para1	In addition to Varnish, we recommend that your developer enable Redis for session and cache storage. Redis helps offload database strain by storing sessions and cache in memory, which speeds up data retrieval and reduces server load.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip2Para2	Why Varnish and Redis? This combination improves both Magento site responsiveness during traffic spikes and backend performance. Net result: a smoother experience for users and administrators.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip3Title	3. Choose Magento Specialist Hosting
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip3Para1Before	Magento is a resource-intensive platform, so it benefits hugely from dedicated, optimised hosting environments. For this reason, consider switching to a Magento specialist hosting provider like
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip3Para1After	, UKFast, or Corefinity. Unlike 'we do everything' providers, they'll know the platform inside and out, and can offer support and infrastructure tailored to Magento performance optimisation.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip3Para2	Bonus tip: make sure your current provider has enabled either Varnish or Redis, and that they're using the right server capacity for your traffic.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip4Title	4. Use a CDN Like Cloudflare
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip4Para1	A Content Delivery Network (CDN) stores your site's static assets (like images, CSS, and JS) on multiple servers around the world. When customers visit your site, the assets load from the nearest server, reducing latency (load time) and improving your Magento Core Web Vitals scores, all of which help your sales margin.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip4Para2Before	At Develo, we use
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip4Para2After	because it's simple to set up, reliable, and has a generous free tier.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip5Title	5. Switch to 'On Schedule' Indexing
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip5Para	Magento lets you choose when data is indexed, either immediately ('on save') or 'on schedule'. For most stores, 'on schedule' indexing is faster and more efficient, reducing admin load and improving user experience (UX), especially for customers browsing large product catalogues.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip6Title	6. Optimise Images and Lazy-Load Below the Fold
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip6Para1	Does your Magento site have large images? If so, they may be slow to load. To correct this, advise your developer to implement lazy-loading for images that appear further down the page ('below the fold') and use formats like WebP. This reduces page weight, improves your Core Web Vitals test scores, and, most importantly, keeps your conversion rates on track.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip6Para2Before	We recommend tools like
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip6Para2After	to automate this process. Also, consider hosting videos externally (on platforms like YouTube or Vimeo) where they're optimised for delivery.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip7Title	7. Review and Reduce Third-Party Scripts
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip7Para1Before	Another way to optimise Magento speed is to review (and, if necessary, remove) site extensions and third-party scripts, especially those added via GTM or directly into Magento. Rooting these out is easy with
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip7Para1Middle	or
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip7Para1After	, and will improve site load times.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip7Para2	Remember: if a script is only required on a single page (such as a PDP or checkout), there's no need to load it sitewide.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip8Title	8. Clean Up and Optimise Your Magento Database
develoContent.blogDetail.body.tip8Para	Task your developer with regularly cleaning and optimising your Magento database. This will stop unused data, including logs and expired quotes, from building up over time. This will improve your Magento site's optimisation and speed up customer queries.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusTitle	Bonus Tips for Magento 2.4 and Beyond
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem1Strong	Use Hyva Themes:
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem1Before	The
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem1After	is an excellent choice for business owners seeking faster-than-Luma speeds and outstanding Core Web Vitals test results. The theme enhances web performance, reduces developer complexity, and improves UX. It's a smart, future-ready upgrade for any Magento store.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem2Strong	Test regularly:
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem2Before	Run monthly speed tests using tools like
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem2Middle	, and
develoContent.blogDetail.body.bonusItem2After	.
develoContent.blogDetail.body.finalTitle	Magento Site Optimisation: Final Thoughts
develoContent.blogDetail.body.finalParaBefore	Magento site optimisation is an ongoing investment. With the right setup and regular reviews, you can keep your store fast, secure, and ahead of the competition, as demonstrated by our work with
develoContent.blogDetail.body.finalParaAfter	.

develoContent.privacy.body.collectTitle	Information We Collect
develoContent.privacy.body.collectIntro	We collect information in the following ways:
develoContent.privacy.body.collectProvidedTitle	Information You Provide
develoContent.privacy.body.collectProvidedPara	When you contact us through forms or communications, we collect information such as your name, email address, phone number, company details, and any other information you choose to provide.
develoContent.privacy.body.collectAutoTitle	Automatic Information
develoContent.privacy.body.collectAutoPara	We automatically collect certain information about your device and how you interact with our website, including IP address, browser type, pages visited, and time spent on pages.
develoContent.privacy.body.collectCookiesTitle	Cookies
develoContent.privacy.body.collectCookiesPara	We use cookies to enhance your browsing experience. See our Cookies Policy section below for more details.
develoContent.privacy.body.useTitle	How We Use Your Information
develoContent.privacy.body.useIntro	We use the information we collect to:
develoContent.privacy.body.useItem1	Respond to your inquiries and provide customer support
develoContent.privacy.body.useItem2	Send you requested information about our services
develoContent.privacy.body.useItem3	Improve our website and services
develoContent.privacy.body.useItem4	Comply with legal obligations
develoContent.privacy.body.useItem5	Prevent fraud and ensure security
develoContent.privacy.body.securityTitle	Data Protection & Security
develoContent.privacy.body.securityPara	We implement appropriate technical and organizational measures to protect your personal information against unauthorized access, alteration, disclosure, or destruction. However, no method of transmission over the Internet is completely secure.
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsTitle	Your Rights
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsIntro	You have the right to:
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem1	Access your personal information
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem2	Correct inaccurate information
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem3	Request deletion of your information
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem4	Opt-out of marketing communications
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem5	Data portability
develoContent.privacy.body.rightsItem6	Withdraw consent at any time
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesTitle	Cookies Policy
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesIntro	We use cookies to improve your experience on our website. Cookies are small files stored on your device that help us remember your preferences and understand how you use our site.
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesTypesTitle	Types of Cookies We Use
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesEssential	Essential Cookies:
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesEssentialDesc	Required for website functionality
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesAnalytics	Analytics Cookies:
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesAnalyticsDesc	Help us understand how you use our site
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesMarketing	Marketing Cookies:
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesMarketingDesc	Used to deliver relevant content and advertisements
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesManageTitle	Managing Cookies
develoContent.privacy.body.cookiesManagePara	You can control cookies through your browser settings. Most browsers allow you to refuse cookies or alert you when a cookie is being sent.
develoContent.privacy.body.thirdPartyTitle	Third-Party Links
develoContent.privacy.body.thirdPartyPara	Our website may contain links to third-party websites. We are not responsible for the privacy practices of other websites and encourage you to review their privacy policies.

develoContent.contact.faq.heading	Frequently Asked Questions
develoContent.contact.faq.item1.question	How do we get started?
develoContent.contact.faq.item1.answer	The first step is a quick call so we can understand your product concept, target market and volume expectations. It is also a chance for you to see how our OEM/ODM process works, from specification review to sample approval and mass production.
develoContent.contact.faq.item2.question	What are your minimum order quantities?
develoContent.contact.faq.item2.answer	We support low minimum order quantities. Our no-MOQ program lets you test the market with small batches first, then scale production as your brand grows — without heavy upfront inventory risk.
develoContent.contact.faq.item3.question	How long does it take to get a sample?
develoContent.contact.faq.item3.answer	For existing models, samples ship within 3-5 business days. For custom-designed devices, prototypes typically take 2-4 weeks depending on the complexity of the design, tooling and features.
develoContent.contact.faq.item4.question	How do we communicate during a project?
develoContent.contact.faq.item4.answer	You get a dedicated account manager with direct communication through email, WhatsApp or WeChat. We provide regular production updates with photos and video reports so you always know where your project stands.
develoContent.contact.faq.item5.question	Can we visit your factory?
develoContent.contact.faq.item5.answer	Yes. We are based in Shenzhen, China, and welcome factory visits. You can walk through our production lines, QC lab and warehouse — most projects begin with a video factory tour, with in-person visits arranged when helpful.
develoContent.contact.faq.item6.question	How much does an IPL device cost?
develoContent.contact.faq.item6.answerBefore	Pricing depends on specifications — energy output, features, materials and packaging — as well as order volume. Share your requirements and we will provide a detailed quote after spec review. For a full overview of how we work, see our
develoContent.contact.faq.item6.answerLink	OEM/ODM page
develoContent.contact.faq.item6.answerAfter	.

develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.process.steps.hypothesis.title	Hypothesis & prioritise
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.process.steps.hypothesis.description	We translate our findings into clear, actionable improvements. Each recommendation is prioritised using RICE scoring so your investment is focused on changes that will make the greatest impact first.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.process.steps.design.title	Design & validate
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.process.steps.design.description	Our UX and UI designers create visual solutions in Figma. Validation may include prototype reviews, usability testing or A/B testing depending on the scale of the update.

develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.process.steps.implement.description	Once designs are approved, our development team builds the updates. We monitor impact through analytics, heatmaps and user feedback, refining changes where needed.

develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.label	FAQ's
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q1.question	What is included in a UX improvement project?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q1.answer	As a Birmingham-based UX design agency, we begin with a review of user behaviour and site data to understand where your eCommerce experience can improve. From there, we design and test solutions that focus on usability, clarity, navigation and conversion.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q2.question	How do you decide which improvements to make first?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q2.answer	Every opportunity is prioritised using RICE scoring so the most impactful changes are tackled early. This ensures quick wins that produce measurable results.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q3.question	How do you measure success?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q3.answer	We compare metrics such as conversion rate, revenue per session and engagement before and after updates. This data-led approach lets us see what is working and continually refine your store experience.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q4.question	Can you support ongoing UX optimisation?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q4.answer	Yes. Through our Magento support packages, we continue to improve UX, CRO and performance over time. Most clients treat us as a long-term partner for design and development improvements.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q5.question	Do you only work on Magento sites?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q5.answer	Magento and Adobe Commerce are our core platforms, but our UX and UI process works across any eCommerce website. As a UX design agency in Birmingham, we combine design, development and data to improve performance on any platform.
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q6.question	What kind of ROI can we expect?
develoSocial.serviceUiuxPage.faq.q6.answer	While every store is different, most clients see noticeable improvements in conversion and revenue within the first few months. We prioritise changes that offer meaningful improvement quickly, giving you maximum return on your investment.

develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.label	FAQ's
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q1.question	Can you take over an existing Magento or Adobe Commerce site?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q1.answer	We regularly onboard Magento, Adobe Commerce and Hyva stores. Whether you need quick fixes, better performance or a long-term roadmap, we can stabilise and strengthen your site.
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q2.question	What makes you different from other Magento agencies?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q2.answer	We're an experienced, in-house team focused on clear communication and measurable results. You work directly with developers, designers and account managers who understand your goals and stay invested in your success, not outsourced teams or layers of account handling.
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q3.question	How do you approach a new development project?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q3.answer	Every engagement starts with discovery. We learn about your systems, customers and commercial goals, then create detailed plans, wireframes and technical specifications. This ensures development runs smoothly and your final solution is aligned with both technical requirements and user needs.
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q4.question	Do you provide ongoing support after launch?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q4.answer	We offer flexible Magento and Adobe Commerce support packages with clear SLAs. Support includes maintenance, security patching, speed optimisation, Hyva upgrades and ongoing development so your site continues to evolve.
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q5.question	How can you help my website grow?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q5.answer	We provide ongoing optimisation, including conversion rate optimisation (CRO), UX improvements, analytics, SEO, Magento performance optimisation and personalised user experiences. Our goal is to improve speed, clarity and conversion across every interaction.
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q6.question	Can Magento integrate with my existing systems?
develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q6.answer	Absolutely. We integrate Magento and Adobe Commerce with ERP, CRM, payment gateways, shipping tools, marketing platforms and specialist B2B systems. We support both custom integrations and connections with major third-party providers.

develoSocial.serviceTechPartnerPage.faq.q7.answer	We deliver full eCommerce builds, platform migrations, Hyva upgrades, performance improvements, UX design, ongoing development and long-term support retainers for Magento, Adobe Commerce and Hyva stores.

productDetail.euno.story.kicker	From the Factory Floor
productDetail.euno.story.title	Why This Design Became a Niche King — and Why Most Copies Fail
productDetail.euno.story.paragraphs[0]	Between 2021 and 2025, this compact single-lamp design quietly became one of the most successful at-home IPL hair removal devices in the world. A wave of direct-to-consumer brands — Kenzzi, HappySkinCo, LuxSkin, MandySkin, RoseSkinCo among them — built entire businesses on it, pairing the device with influencer campaigns that spread across YouTube, Instagram, and Facebook. At its peak, related videos alone drew tens of millions of views. The reason it worked was simple: a clean, single-function device, in black, pink, and white, at a price point first-time buyers could say yes to.
productDetail.euno.story.paragraphs[1]	Then the copies arrived. The design was easy to replicate on the outside, and a wave of sellers did exactly that — swapping the quartz lamp, the optical filter, the reflector cup, the capacitors, and the power adapter for cheaper parts, driving build cost down to under 30% of the original. The result was a flood of devices that could still flash but broke within weeks. They gave a great design a bad reputation.
productDetail.euno.story.paragraphs[2]	That history is why a durable, effective first-generation unit is so hard to find today, and it is why we keep the original spec — quartz lamp rated for 999,999 flashes, UV-cut filter, certified adapter, 2-year warranty — intact on this platform. If you are considering this design for your own brand, the advice from the factory floor is simple: do not compete on the copy. Compete on the spec, the certification, and the supply chain that keeps the copies from becoming your reputation.
productDetail.euno.story.cta.label	Talk to us about building this platform for your brand
productDetail.euno.story.cta.href	/contact

productDetail.index.summary.description	iShine manufactures a full range of IPL hair removal devices for beauty and lifestyle brands. Our home IPL device portfolio spans ice-feeling cooling platforms (Venus, Lumi 2), smart app-connected IPL devices (Lumi, Hestia), portable travel IPL devices (Euno), salon-grade professional IPL devices (Themis, Alpha), and luxury wooden-finish editions (Golden). Each IPL hair removal device platform is engineered for OEM/ODM customization — logo printing, bilingual user manuals, custom box inserts, and export packaging — so a brand can launch a home IPL device line quickly without designing hardware from scratch. Whether you need an entry-level IPL device, a premium app-controlled model, or a professional-grade unit, we build the device behind your brand.

productDetail.index.faq.items[4].question	What is the difference between an IPL device and a laser hair removal device?
productDetail.index.faq.items[4].answer	An IPL hair removal device emits broad-spectrum light pulses; a laser emits a single wavelength. Both achieve permanent hair reduction. Our guide explains the difference for your customers.
